# Session Handoff

## Current position

Honc Telegram deployment, FastMCP stdio lifecycle, and UuMA control guard repair turn: 2026-10-07.
This turn PROGRESS: resolved honc startup failure and control guard interception on Telegram.
1. Root cause 1 (honc MCP entrypoint): src/uuma/honc/mcp.py was missing main() and if __name__ == '__main__': main(). When spawned by Hermes via stdio subprocess, the process exited immediately on import without serving MCP stdio, causing Hermes to park honc-execution with 'Connection closed'. Added def main(): mcp.run() and if __name__ == '__main__': main(). Unit test test_honc_mcp_main added and verified.
2. Root cause 2 (uuma_control_guard preflight crash): In integrations/hermes/uuma_control_guard/__init__.py, _DOMAIN_PREFLIGHT[profile] was defined for honc, but the arguments dictionary lookup omitted 'honc', raising unhandled KeyError: 'honc' in _health_context. When _health_context failed, Direct Run registration and domain preflight were aborted, causing _guard_specialist_output to fail-closed and return the intercepted error message to Telegram. Fixed by adding 'honc': {} to arguments, moving honc_guidance to outer scope, updating reversible_specialist to include honc with risk_level='REVERSIBLE', and tracking honc_execution tool calls in _post_tool_call. Unit test test_honc_auto_registers_and_checks_execution_identity added and verified.
3. Updated tests/test_control_plane.py: updated default agent count assertions from 6 to 7 for honc.
4. Verification: Full pytest suite ran and passed 439/439 tests (0 failures). Ruff check passed on src, tests, and integrations/hermes/uuma_control_guard.
5. Profile deployment and live gateway restart:
   - Deployed updated guard and skills to C:\Users\Alandelone\AppData\Local\hermes\profiles\honc via python scripts/deploy-honc-profile.py --apply.
   - Terminated old detached gateway PID 43452 and restarted scheduled task Hermes_Gateway (PID 65532).
   - Confirmed in profiles/honc/logs/agent.log: honc-execution registered all 14 tools and uuma-worker registered 9 tools (total 23 tools), state connected.
   - Confirmed in hermes/logs/agent.log: telegram connected (profile: honc) with bot token active.

# Session Handoff

## Current position

工作包 B（执行账本）圆满完成：在 C:\Users\Alandelone\CodeSpace_Local\UuMA 搭建了 uuma.honc 模块架构，建立 honc.db 独立数据隔离迁移模型与事件账本，并实现全套核心 FastMCP 工具与单元测试。

【架构与设计落地】
1. Settings 隔离扩展 (src/uuma/settings.py)：
   - 增加 honc_data_dir 与 honc_database_path()，确保 honc 执行数据存放在独立 UUMA_DATA_DIR/honc/honc.db，与 uuma.db、wisdom.db、state.db 及 graph.sqlite3 严格物理隔离，绝不跨库连接。
2. 数据模型 (src/uuma/honc/models.py)：
   - 定义 TrustedInput、TaskSnapshot、Round、RoundSegment、RoundStatus、ActualKind、EventType、HabitDefinition、CompletionAssessment、AssessmentResult、OutboxItem、OutboxStatus、ProjectionBinding、DailyReport。
   - 编写 get_timezone 助手函数，为 Windows 环境在无 tzdata 依赖时提供 Asia/Kuala_Lumpur (UTC+8) 的数学等价安全回退，防止 ZoneInfoNotFoundError。
3. 执行账本存储层 (src/uuma/honc/store.py)：
   - SQLite 模式 v1 迁移系统；启用 WAL 模式、PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON 与 PRAGMA synchronous = FULL。
   - trusted_inputs 表通过 source_digest 实现用户消息输入幂等去重。
   - rounds 表通过部分唯一索引 (idx_rounds_active_user) 严密约束每个用户在 PREPARED/RUNNING/PAUSED/AWAITING_CHECK 状态下最多仅有一个活动回合。
   - round_segments 记录每段开始与结束时间，暂停时间不计入执行时长。
   - execution_events 附带 SQLite 触发器（execution_events_no_update / execution_events_no_delete），物理保证执行事实追加不可篡改。
   - 习惯打卡、核对评估、outbox 幂等队列、投影绑定及基于 Asia/Kuala_Lumpur 日历日的日报聚合。
4. Yonc Graph 执行客户端 (src/uuma/honc/graph_client.py)：
   - 适配工作包 A 对接契约：POST /api/v2/execution/action-decisions、GET /api/v2/execution/receipts/{operation_id}、POST /api/v2/execution/focus-events。
5. 核心业务服务 (src/uuma/honc/service.py)：
   - 协调回合生命周期（prepare/start/pause/resume/end/cancel）与修订号冲突校验。
   - 未知时间严禁编造（无输入时长时强制 actual_kind=unknown）。
   - 补记（backfill）保留真实发生时间与记录时间的双轨分离。
   - 依据 done_when 准则评估执行成果，满足条件时向 Graph 提交完成并记录回执。
   - 纠正记录追加修正事实并保留原始历史，支持反向补偿操作。
6. FastMCP 语义工具 (src/uuma/honc/mcp.py)：
   - FastMCP("honc-execution") 暴露 10 个核心工具：honc_status, honc_get_context, honc_record_execution, honc_control_round, honc_check_habit, honc_assess_completion, honc_apply_completion, honc_correct_record, honc_get_report, honc_reconcile_updates。
   - 统一输出规范，避免以一句模糊的 Done 代表所有结果。

【验证与回归测试】
- tests/test_honc_ledger.py: 12 个测试全数通过（模式迁移、去重、不可变触发器、回合生命周期、修订冲突、未知时间保护、补记双轨、习惯打卡、验收标准核对、纠正历史链、日报聚合、重启恢复）。
- tests/test_honc_mcp.py: 5 个端到端 MCP 测试全数通过（FastMCP 工具集、FakeGraphClient 对接、focus 联动、完成提交与回执、补偿与对账）。
- 全量 17 个测试在 2.45s 内全部绿色通过；Ruff 代码检查 100 字符限制与严格类型规则零警告零错误。

# Session Handoff

## Current position

Resolved wisdom-oldman execution and guard interception issues, and reverted Kanban board to Phase 5 baseline.

Key actions completed:
1. uuma_control_guard fixes:
   - Updated integrations/hermes/uuma_control_guard/__init__.py to detect Kanban worker execution via HERMES_KANBAN_TASK env var, work kanban task prompt prefix, source=kanban, or t_<hex> task ID pattern.
   - In _health_context: bypassed direct-chat preflight and Direct Run auto-registration for Kanban workers, setting state.is_kanban=True, direct_run_id=kanban:<task_id>, and wisdom_intent_resolved=True.

2. wisdom-oldman profile configuration:
   - In C:\Users\Alandelone\AppData\Local\hermes\profiles\wisdom-oldman\config.yaml, added tools.tool_search.enabled: off (matching brainstormer, forge-lab-bot, and yonc), preventing MCP tools from being masked behind progressive tool search.
